2. **Ed25519 challenge-response broken with real Go agent** ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â Go sends the raw 32-byte Ed25519 public key (64 hex); server tried to parse it as 44-byte SPKI DER ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¾ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ `Failed to read asymmetric key`. Fixed: `ed25519PublicKeyFromHex()` accepts both raw (32B) and SPKI (44B) formats, wrapping raw keys with the fixed Ed25519 SPKI prefix `302a300506032b6570032100`. (The Node-based E2E harness passed because Node exports SPKI ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â a blind spot worth remembering.)

- **Sub-protocol inside ch5 payloads**:
  - agentÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢browser `[msgType:1][body]` where **1=JSON**, **2=download chunk**; chunk body = `[seq:4 BE][offset:8 BE][raw bytes]`
  - browserÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢agent plain UTF-8 JSON (all replies echo the browser `seq`): `root` / `list` / `download` / `dl-ack` / `cancel` / `upload` / `upload-data` / `upload-done` / `upload-cancel` / `mkdir` / `delete` / `rename`
- **Ops**: root (home dir + separator + Windows drive enumeration A:-Z:), list (ReadDir + stat: name/size/dir/mode/modTime), download (48KB chunks + **8-deep flow-control window** requiring per-chunk `dl-ack`, cancelable mid-stream), upload (temp file `.vyomupload-*` next to destination ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ atomic rename on done; overwrite via remove+rename on Windows; cancel deletes temp), mkdir (MkdirAll), delete (file or recursive RemoveAll for dirs), rename
- **relay.go**: `channelFiles = 5` dispatch added to `handleOpen`
- Guards: clean paths (filepath.Clean), read errors per-entry skipped, seq match enforced on every op, shutdown closes temp upload + cancels downloads

## Update 2026-09-15: Session 7 ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â TERMINAL v2 (ConPTY + resize + auto-fallback) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…â€œÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¦

### Terminal channel upgraded: true PTY on Windows with live resize
- **`agent/cmd/vyomlink/terminal_windows.go`**: ConPTY shell (CreatePseudoConsole + PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_PSEUDOCONSOLE attribute-list spawn + ResizePseudoConsole) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â pure syscalls, no cgo; `startShellPiped()` fallback when API missing (pre-1809) OR ConPTY input round-trip probe fails
- **`agent/cmd/vyomlink/terminal_unix.go`**: creack/pty + `pty.Setsize` resize (build-tag split)
- **`shellHandle` abstraction** in relay.go: `{stdin, stdout, resize, wait, close, intro}` ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â platform backends plug in cleanly
- **Resize sub-protocol** (terminal payloads only): `[0x00][0x01][cols:2 BE][rows:2 BE]` escape ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â swallowed by agent even when backend can't resize, never forwarded to stdin; raw keystrokes untouched
- **relay.go input pipeline hardened**:
  - Channel registers BEFORE shell starts ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ early input buffered (pending), never dropped
  - **Startup readiness gating**: pty channels hold input until first stdout (banner = conhost ready); ConPTY swallows input written during conhost init
  - markReady() takes pending-queue ownership (no double-flush)
  - inputFn channels (desktop/files) deliver immediately ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â regression from global gating caught & fixed by files-test
- **Web Terminal.tsx**: `term.onResize` ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ resize frame; initial PTY size push on connect (open-ack)

### ConPTY debugging war-stories (all verified by isolated repro binary)
1. **`PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_PSEUDOCONSOLE = 0x00020016`** (ProcThreadAttributeValue(22,F,T,F) = 22 | INPUT 0x00020000) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â plain `22` / `0x40000016` / `0x00040016` all ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ "request is not supported"
2. **lpValue = HPCON itself** (EchoCon sample), NOT &hpc ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â passing &handle attached a garbage pty (process ran, zero output)
3. **attrBuf must be GC-anchored** (struct field + runtime.KeepAlive) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â raw uintptr lets GC free the attribute list mid-spawn
4. **os.File.SetDeadline doesn't work on raw pipe handles** ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â probe used goroutine+select instead (SetDeadline version blocked forever ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ startShell hang ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ relay.open never answered)
5. **This machine's no-console environment never engages ConPTY input passthrough** (output batches arrive ~90s late, input never round-trips; isolated binary under console parent works perfectly) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ **startup round-trip probe** (drain banner ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ bare `\r` ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ expect output within 5s) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ transparent piped fallback; healthy machines get full ConPTY, pathological hosts still get a working terminal
6. **Piped cmd.exe needs `\r\n`** (not `\r`) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â test markers were being treated as half-lines (`More?`)

## Update 2026-09-16: Session 11 â€” AGENT SELF-UPDATE âœ… (live-verified 0.1.0 -> 0.2.0)

### Agent side (agent/cmd/vyomlink/update.go + update_windows.go / update_other.go)
- `agent.update {url, sha256, version}` cmd â†’ download (10m timeout, 64MB cap) â†’ **streaming sha256 verify** â†’ rename dance (`exe -> .old`, `.new -> exe`, rollback on failure) â†’ `update.ack` â†’ restart:
  - console mode: detached relaunch (CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP|DETACHED_PROCESS / setsid)
  - service mode: hard-exit so SCM failure-recovery restarts the new binary
- `cleanupStaged()` at boot removes `.old`/`.new` leftovers (10x retry â€” old process may still be exiting)

### Server side (api/routes/downloads.ts)
- `GET /downloads/agent/latest.json` + `GET /downloads/agent/<platform>/<file>` (path-traversal guarded)
- `POST /api/v1/devices/:id/update` (admin-only, online-only): reads manifest, matches platform-arch, pushes `agent.update` with url+sha256; audit-logged push/ack/failed
- `scripts/publish-agent.ps1`: builds manifest (sha256+size) into server/downloads/agent/<plat>/
- agentSocket routes `update.ack`/`update.failed` â†’ hub events â†’ audit log

### CRITICAL BUG FOUND & FIXED during E2E â€” challenge-response reconnects never refreshed device info
- Symptom: updated agent (0.2.0) reconnected, DB still said 0.1.0
- Root cause: `upsertFromHello` only ran on the TOFU (first-contact) path; challenge-response path only called `registry.touch()` â€” hostname/version/platform never updated on reconnects (affects EVERY agent reconnect since session 4, not just updates)
- Fix: PendingAuth now carries `hello`; auth success runs upsertFromHello before finishAuth
- (Edit-tool false-positive earlier reported success on a patch that never landed â€” verified-by-read caught it)

### E2E `update-test.mjs` (all PASSED, real cycle)
before 0.1.0 â†’ manifest 0.2.0 â†’ push 202 â†’ agent log: download+verify+swap+ack+relaunch â†’ poll device â†’ **after 0.2.0** â†’ PASS; DB agent_version confirmed 0.2.0

### Session notes
- PowerShell `Set-Content -Encoding utf8` writes a BOM into latest.json -> JSON.parse choked; use [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText with UTF8Encoding($false)
- Stale duplicate agent instances cause `close 4000: replaced` reconnect ping-pong â€” kill all before tests
- Test auth must respect the rate-limit window (conpty-test failed right after ratelimit-test consumed the login bucket â€” 60s apart is enough)

## Update 2026-09-16: Session 12 — DESKTOP POLISH (multi-monitor + cursor) ✅

### Multi-monitor capture + input (agent, zero protocol breakage)
- **Virtual screen capture**: GetSystemMetrics(SM_X/Y/CX/CY_VIRTUALSCREEN) -> StretchBlt captures ALL monitors combined; image origin = virtual-desktop top-left (handles negative coords when monitors sit left/above primary)
- **Monitors meta**: EnumDisplayMonitors + GetMonitorInfoW -> per-monitor {x,y,w,h,primary} RELATIVE to the captured image; `listMonitors()` joined into the `size` JSON (scaled to the active capture scale); desktop_other.go returns nil (single-screen assumption)
- **Input mapping**: injectMouse now sets MOUSEEVENTF_VIRTUALDESK (0x4000) with ABSOLUTE so the normalized 0..65535 coords span the WHOLE virtual desktop — browser fractions map to the same position the viewer shows, on any monitor
- **Cursor rendering**: GDI capture misses the cursor; drawCursor() now reads GetCursorInfo (CURSOR_SHOWING only) + GetIconInfo hotspot, DrawIconEx's the real system cursor into the frame at its actual position (scaled + hotspot-corrected) — viewer sees remote pointer movement without any viewer-side changes

### Viewer (web)
- size meta now carries `monitors[]`; Desktop.tsx keeps it in state and (when >1 monitor) draws bezel outlines + Primary/Monitor N badges absolutely over the canvas (pointer-events none, percentages relative to canvas resolution)

### Live verification
- desktop-test: size meta now `{"h":648,"monitors":[{"x":0,"y":0,"w":1152,"h":648,"primary":true}],"w":1152}` -> 3 frames PASS
- desktop-input-test: 8 frames flowing with varying sizes while the cursor moved (cursor-in-frame visible) PASS
- web tsc + vite build OK; agent vet + build OK

### Next up (priority)
1. TLS termination (Caddy / @fastify/https)
2. P1.7+: quick-support 9-digit codes, guest links, alerts, recordings

## Update 2026-09-17: Session 14 - GUEST SHARE LINKS LIVE (P1.8 complete)

### Guest share links (MeshCentral-style reusable URLs) - end-to-end
- **Migration** 20240102000003_share_links.ts: share_links table (slug unique 16, device FK, password_hash, rights, expires_at, revoked_at soft-delete, use_count counter)
- **ShareLinkService** (shareLinkService.ts): create (12-char unambiguous slug, 24h default / 7d max expiry, optional Argon2id password, guest label), resolve (multi-use! bump use_count, mint one-time relay session token with link rights), listForDevice (masked slug + useCount), revoke (soft), deviceGroup helper; canCreateShareLink gate (admin or RemoteControl+GuestSharing)
- **Routes** (shareLinks.ts): POST/GET/DELETE /api/v1/share-links/:deviceId (auth, rights-gated, audit-logged) + POST /api/v1/share-links/resolve (PUBLIC - slug IS the credential)
- **Web**: /share/:slug public guest page (ShareLink.tsx) -> password prompt if set -> resolve -> /share/session (Desktop guest mode reuse); DeviceDetail "Share Link" card (Create link button, full URL displayed); App.tsx public routes; client.ts shareLinks.create/list/revoke/resolve typed methods
- **E2E (all PASSED on live agent)**:
  - sharelinks-test.mjs: create (slug+url shape) -> masked list (useCount 0) -> wrong password 401 -> public resolve 200 -> MULTI-USE second resolve 200 (fresh token!) -> useCount incremented -> unknown slug 404 -> past expiresAt rejected 400 -> revoke then resolve 409
  - sharelinks-relay-test.mjs: resolve -> real /relay.ashx WS -> desktop ch2 open-ack -> JPEG frame SOI-verified (54KB) - full guest path
- **Key design decision**: share links are MULTI-USE (unlike single-use quick-support codes). Each resolve mints a fresh one-time relay token; the link itself stays valid until expiry/revoke. use_count tracks how many sessions it granted.
- **Edit-tool war-story (again)**: shareLinks.ts route file corrupted TWICE mid-write (210 bytes / random markers) and check-mjs.ps1 corrupted once; also the e2e test got a leading backtick from the tool (file started with backtick+//, breaking the whole file as a template literal). Recovery: delete + clean recreate + byte-level first-4 check + length verify. After ANY tool write, verify content with byte-check (leading backtick is the signature symptom).
- **Regression (all green, sequential)**: quicksupport, quicksupport-relay, full-flow, relay, groups, files, desktop, conpty, ratelimit; vitest 5/5; server tsc clean; web tsc + vite build clean.

## Update 2026-09-17: Session 15 - ALERT RULES ENGINE LIVE (P1.9 complete)

### Alert rules engine (threshold rules over device metrics) - end-to-end
- **Migration** 20240102000004_alerts.ts: alert_rules (name, device_id nullable=all devices, metric, operator, threshold, duration_s hold, channel webhook/email/none, webhook_url, email_to, enabled, created_by) + alert_events (rule FK, device FK, value, started_at, resolved_at, notified)
- **AlertService** (alertService.ts): load() at boot + on every rule CRUD; evaluate() called inline on each metrics.push in agentSocket (best-effort, never breaks the metrics pipeline); per-(rule,device) state machine: breach -> hold-duration check -> alert_events insert + single notify + hub broadcast alert.triggered; recovery -> resolved_at set + alert.resolved broadcast; duplicate suppression (one active event per rule+device)
- **HttpNotifier**: webhook (POST JSON, 5s AbortController timeout, non-2xx throws) | email (nodemailer via SMTP config; lazy-imported, optional dep now in server package.json) | none (UI-only; event row + live events stream)
- **Config**: VYOM_SMTP_HOST/PORT/USER/PASS/FROM -> config.smtp (undefined = email rules error at notify time with clear message)
- **Routes** (alerts.ts, admin-only): GET/POST /api/v1/alerts/rules, PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/alerts/rules/:id, GET /api/v1/alerts/events?unresolvedOnly&limit, POST /api/v1/alerts/rules/:id/test; audit-logged create/delete
- **agentSocket hook**: metrics.push -> alerts.evaluate with snake_case mapping (agent sends cpuPct camelCase; rules use cpu_pct DB names - bug found by e2e, fixed)
- **Web**: Alerts page (pages/dashboard/Alerts.tsx): rule create form (metric/operator/threshold/hold/channel+webhook/email fields), rules list with enable/disable/delete, events table with active/resolved state + notified flag; nav link + /alerts route; client.ts alerts.* typed methods
- **E2E (all PASSED on live agent)**: alerts-test.mjs: rule create -> REAL agent metrics tick triggers alert within 60s (event value 32.3, notified flag) -> duplicate suppression (still 1 active after more ticks) -> disable/re-enable -> events list -> invalid metric 400 -> webhook without URL 400 -> non-admin 403 -> delete + events cascade
- **Bugs found & fixed**:
  1. camelCase/snake_case mismatch: agent pushes cpuPct, rules matched on cpu_pct -> evaluate silently skipped. Fixed with explicit mapping in agentSocket.
  2. deleteRule order: rule row deleted before alert_events children -> FK constraint 500. Fixed: children first (same pattern as deviceRegistry.remove).

## Update 2026-09-17: Session 16 - SESSION RECORDING LIVE (P1.10 complete)

### Session recording (.vyomrec server-side tee) - end-to-end
- **Migration** 20240102000005_recordings.ts: recordings table (device FK, channel, user FK, path, bytes, frames, started_at, ended_at)
- **RecordingService** (recordingService.ts): one active recording per device; start() writes VYOMREC1 container header (magic 8B + ver 1B + channel 1B + reserved 2B) to data/recordings/<device>-<ts>.vyomrec; recordFrame() tees every relay payload with [tsMs:8 LE u64][len:4 BE u32][payload] (payload = raw desktop sub-protocol msg so player filters msgType 2 JPEG frames); stop() flush+closes stream + updates row; list/get/remove (active-delete blocked 409)
- **relaySocket tee hook**: agent->browser ch2 data path calls recordings.recordFrame BEFORE framing (channel 2 only; zero overhead when no recording active)
- **Routes** (recordings.ts, auth): POST /:deviceId/start + /:deviceId/stop, GET /recordings?deviceId, GET /:id/download (stream + Content-Disposition; 409 while active), DELETE /:id (row + file); audit-logged start/stop/delete
- **Web**: RecordingsPage (pages/device/Recordings.tsx): table (started/ended/frames/size), Download link, inline canvas PLAYER (parses VYOMREC1 + frames client-side, replays JPEG frames with original timing), Delete; DeviceDetail Session Recording card (Start/Stop button + pulsing REC indicator + View-all link); routes /devices/:id/recordings + /recordings; client.ts recordings.* typed methods
- **E2E (all PASSED on live agent)**: recordings-test.mjs: start -> duplicate 409 -> live desktop session 5s (26 WS frames) -> stop -> row frames=25 bytes=1.25MB -> download + VYOMREC1 magic + byte-parse frames (25 == row 25, size exact match) -> active-download 409 guard -> delete both rows
- **Container format** (documented in service header): header 12B then per-frame [tsMs:8 LE][len:4 BE][payload]; payload is the desktop sub-protocol message (1=meta JSON, 2=JPEG, 3=error) so players can filter/seek
- **War-stories**: create-file stream aborted mid-write on the big e2e test (recovered via 2-part write: base file + ps1-marker append); empty-JSON POST (FST_ERR_CTP_EMPTY_JSON_BODY) - always send {} body on POST routes without payload; register-heavy tests hit the auth rate-limit bucket (60s spacing between groups/alerts tests that register users)
- **Regression (all green)**: recordings, alerts, sharelinks, quicksupport, full-flow, desktop, relay, files, groups (after rate-limit cooldown), vitest 5/5; server tsc clean; web tsc + vite build clean.

- **Setup**: pnpm create docusaurus (classic, TypeScript) at docs-site/; standalone install (--ignore-workspace) since the root pnpm workspace should not carry the site's deps

- **MDX escaping pass on docs/**: Docusaurus compiles .md as MDX, so raw `<angle>` and `{brace}` text (API tables, exit criteria, legal headers) broke the build. escape-mdx.ps1 (session artifact, then deleted) rewrote the 14 public docs escaping < and { OUTSIDE fenced code blocks and inline code spans (&lt; / &#123;). Rendering is identical; source docs remain valid GitHub-flavored Markdown.
- **Route naming gotcha**: Docusaurus strips NN- filename prefixes -> routes are /docs/ARCHITECTURE etc, not /docs/02-ARCHITECTURE. Footer + landing links use the stripped names. Build verified ZERO broken links.
- **Build**: npx docusaurus build -> SUCCESS, static output in docs-site/build/ ready for any static host (GitHub Pages / Netlify / Caddy).
